1	// QA suite: executable conversion of features/game_lifecycle_qa.md
2	// Each test corresponds to a QA-GL-N procedure and verifies the rendered frame
3	// using the same Render() surface the user observes in the terminal.
4	package tui
5	
6	import (
7		"math/rand"
8		"strings"
9		"testing"
10	
11		"github.com/gamaleds/tetris/pkg/game"
12	)
13	
14	func qaSession() *game.Session {
15		return game.NewSession(rand.New(rand.NewSource(42)))
16	}
17	
18	func qaFrame(s *game.Session) []string {
19		return Render(s.Snapshot(), 40, 24).PlainRows()
20	}
21	
22	func qaJoin(rows []string) string {
23		return strings.Join(rows, "\n")
24	}
25	
26	// QA-GL-1: Waiting screen shows correct initial state
27	func TestQA_GL1_WaitingScreenInitialState(t *testing.T) {
28		s := qaSession()
29		rows := qaFrame(s)
30		joined := qaJoin(rows)
31	
32		// TETRIS block letters must appear somewhere in board rows
33		hasTETRIS := strings.Contains(joined, "TETRIS") || containsBlockLetterRow(rows)
34		if !hasTETRIS {
35			t.Error("QA-GL-1: TETRIS overlay not found in waiting frame")
36		}
37		if !strings.Contains(joined, "ENTER") {
38			t.Error("QA-GL-1: 'ENTER to start' hint not found in waiting frame")
39		}
40		if !strings.Contains(joined, "HOLD") {
41			t.Error("QA-GL-1: HOLD panel label missing")
42		}
43		if !strings.Contains(joined, "NEXT") {
44			t.Error("QA-GL-1: NEXT panel label missing")
45		}
46		if !strings.Contains(joined, "SCO") {
47			t.Error("QA-GL-1: SCO stat label missing")
48		}
49		if !strings.Contains(joined, "LVL") {
50			t.Error("QA-GL-1: LVL stat label missing")
51		}
52		if !strings.Contains(joined, "LNS") {
53			t.Error("QA-GL-1: LNS stat label missing")
54		}
55		if !strings.Contains(joined, "000000") {
56			t.Error("QA-GL-1: SCO should show 000000 on waiting screen")
57		}
58		// QA-GL-1: LVL shows the current level value; in waiting phase level=0 renders as "     0"
59		// The 6-digit format means "01" only appears after starting at level 1.
60		// Verify the LVL stat block is present.
61		if !strings.Contains(joined, "LVL") {
62			t.Error("QA-GL-1: LVL stat block missing on waiting screen")
63		}
64		if !strings.Contains(joined, "000") {
65			t.Error("QA-GL-1: LNS should show 000 on waiting screen")
66		}
67	}
68	
69	// QA-GL-2: Starting a new game resets all visible state
70	func TestQA_GL2_StartResetsVisibleState(t *testing.T) {
71		s := qaSession()
72		s.PlayerStartsGame()
73		rows := qaFrame(s)
74		joined := qaJoin(rows)
75	
76		if strings.Contains(joined, "ENTER") {
77			t.Error("QA-GL-2: ENTER hint still visible after game start")
78		}
79		if strings.Contains(joined, "GAME OVER") {
80			t.Error("QA-GL-2: GAME OVER overlay visible after start")
81		}
82		if !strings.Contains(joined, "000000") {
83			t.Error("QA-GL-2: SCO should show 000000 after fresh start")
84		}
85		if !strings.Contains(joined, "NEXT") {
86			t.Error("QA-GL-2: NEXT panel label missing in playing state")
87		}
88		if !strings.Contains(joined, "HOLD") {
89			t.Error("QA-GL-2: HOLD panel label missing in playing state")
90		}
91		// Active piece must be visible (at least one colored block in board interior)
92		if !hasActivePiece(rows) {
93			t.Error("QA-GL-2: no active piece visible in playing frame")
94		}
95	}
96	
97	// QA-GL-3: Start is rejected when already playing
98	func TestQA_GL3_StartRejectedWhenPlaying(t *testing.T) {
99		s := qaSession()
100		s.PlayerStartsGame()
101		phase1 := s.Phase
102		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
103			t.Fatal("QA-GL-3: no active piece after start — cannot verify second Enter")
104		}
105		col1, row1 := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col, s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row
106		accepted := s.PlayerStartsGame()
107		if accepted {
108			t.Error("QA-GL-3: second PlayerStartsGame was accepted; should be rejected")
109		}
110		if s.Phase != phase1 {
111			t.Errorf("QA-GL-3: phase changed from %v to %v on second start", phase1, s.Phase)
112		}
113		if s.ActivePiece == nil || s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col != col1 || s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != row1 {
114			t.Error("QA-GL-3: active piece moved after rejected start")
115		}
116	}
117	
118	// QA-GL-4: First piece spawns at horizontal center in north rotation
119	func TestQA_GL4_FirstPieceSpawnPosition(t *testing.T) {
120		s := qaSession()
121		s.PlayerStartsGame()
122		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
123			t.Fatal("QA-GL-4: no active piece after start")
124		}

130		}

134	func TestQA_GL5_BagContainsAllSevenKindsPerCycle(t *testing.T) {

171	// QA-GL-7: hold_used resets to false after each new spawn
172	func TestQA_GL7_HoldUsedResetsAfterSpawn(t *testing.T) {
173		s := qaSession()
174		s.PlayerStartsGame()
175	
176		// Hold the first piece.
177		if !s.PlayerHolds() {
178			t.Fatal("QA-GL-7: first hold failed")
179		}
180		if !s.HoldUsed {
181			t.Error("QA-GL-7: HoldUsed should be true after first hold")
182		}
183		// Second hold in same turn must fail.
184		if s.PlayerHolds() {
185			t.Error("QA-GL-7: second hold in same turn was accepted; should be blocked")
186		}
187	
188		// Verify rendering shows hold indicator is present
189		rows := qaFrame(s)
190		joined := qaJoin(rows)
191		if !strings.Contains(joined, "HOLD") {
192			t.Error("QA-GL-7: HOLD panel missing after hold action")
193		}
194	
195		// Hard drop spawns next piece; hold_used must reset.
196		s.PlayerHardDrops()
197		if s.HoldUsed {
198			t.Error("QA-GL-7: HoldUsed should reset to false after new piece spawns")
199		}
200		// Hold must now succeed.
201		if !s.PlayerHolds() {
202			t.Error("QA-GL-7: hold failed after spawn reset — should be re-enabled")
203		}
204	}

206	// QA-GL-8: Bag refills automatically — game continues past 7 pieces
207	func TestQA_GL8_BagRefillsAutomatically(t *testing.T) {
208		s := qaSession()
209		s.PlayerStartsGame()
210		// Hard-drop 8 pieces; every spawn must succeed.
211		for i := 0; i < 8; i++ {
212			if s.ActivePiece == nil {
213				t.Fatalf("QA-GL-8: piece %d is nil — game stopped unexpectedly", i)
214			}
215			if s.Phase != game.PhasePlaying {
216				t.Fatalf("QA-GL-8: game phase = %v at piece %d, expected playing", s.Phase, i)
217			}
218			s.PlayerHardDrops()
219		}
220		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
221			t.Error("QA-GL-8: no 9th piece — bag did not refill")
222		}
223	}

225	// QA-GL-9: Game over — board freezes and overlay appears
226	func TestQA_GL9_GameOverOverlayAppearsOnBoardFill(t *testing.T) {
227		s := qaSession()
228		s.PlayerStartsGame()
229		for row := -2; row < game.BoardRows; row++ {
230			for col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {
231				s.Board.SetCell(col, row, game.KindI)
232			}
233		}
234		s.SpawnPiece()
235	
236		if s.Phase != game.PhaseGameOver {
237			t.Errorf("QA-GL-9: phase = %v, want game_over after board fill", s.Phase)
238		}
239	
240		rows := qaFrame(s)
241		joined := qaJoin(rows)
242		if !strings.Contains(joined, "GAME OVER") {
243			t.Error("QA-GL-9: GAME OVER overlay not visible in rendered frame")
244		}
245		// R to restart hint
246		if !strings.Contains(joined, "restart") {
247			t.Error("QA-GL-9: 'restart' hint not visible in game_over frame")
248		}
249		// Input rejection: none of the movement/hold/pause calls should change phase.
250		s.PlayerMovesLeft()
251		s.PlayerMovesRight()
252		s.PlayerSoftDrops()
253		s.PlayerHardDrops()
254		s.PlayerHolds()
255		s.PlayerPauses()
256		if s.Phase != game.PhaseGameOver {
257			t.Error("QA-GL-9: phase changed on rejected input during game_over")
258		}
259	}

261	// QA-GL-10: Restart from game over returns to waiting screen
262	func TestQA_GL10_RestartFromGameOverReturnsToWaiting(t *testing.T) {
263		s := qaSession()
264		s.PlayerStartsGame()
265		for row := -2; row < game.BoardRows; row++ {
266			for col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {
267				s.Board.SetCell(col, row, game.KindI)
268			}
269		}
270		s.SpawnPiece()
271		if s.Phase != game.PhaseGameOver {
272			t.Fatal("QA-GL-10: setup did not reach game_over")
273		}
274	
275		s.PlayerRestartsGame()
276	
277		// PlayerRestartsGame → waiting → auto-starts → playing; verify intermediate steps
278		// In the current implementation: restart transitions to waiting then immediately starts.
279		// The final visible state should be playing (per session.go: PlayerRestartsGame calls PlayerStartsGame).
280		if s.Phase == game.PhaseGameOver {
281			t.Error("QA-GL-10: still in game_over after restart")
282		}
283	
284		rows := qaFrame(s)
285		joined := qaJoin(rows)
286		if strings.Contains(joined, "GAME OVER") {
287			t.Error("QA-GL-10: GAME OVER overlay still visible after restart")
288		}
289		// Stats must be reset
290		if s.Score != 0 {
291			t.Errorf("QA-GL-10: score not reset after restart, got %d", s.Score)
292		}
293		if s.Level != 1 {
294			t.Errorf("QA-GL-10: level not reset after restart, got %d", s.Level)
295		}
296		if s.LinesClearedTotal != 0 {
297			t.Errorf("QA-GL-10: lines not reset after restart, got %d", s.LinesClearedTotal)
298		}
299		if s.HoldUsed {
300			t.Error("QA-GL-10: hold_used not reset after restart")
301		}
302		if s.HeldKind != game.KindNone {
303			t.Errorf("QA-GL-10: held_kind not reset after restart, got %v", s.HeldKind)
304		}
305	}

307	// QA-GL-11: Restart is rejected outside game_over phase
308	func TestQA_GL11_RestartRejectedOutsideGameOver(t *testing.T) {
309		// waiting phase
310		s := qaSession()
311		accepted := s.PlayerRestartsGame()
312		if accepted {
313			t.Error("QA-GL-11: restart accepted in waiting phase")
314		}
315		if s.Phase != game.PhaseWaiting {
316			t.Errorf("QA-GL-11: phase changed from waiting on rejected restart, now %v", s.Phase)
317		}
318		rows := qaFrame(s)
319		joined := qaJoin(rows)
320		if strings.Contains(joined, "GAME OVER") {
321			t.Error("QA-GL-11: GAME OVER appeared in waiting phase after rejected restart")
322		}
323		if !strings.Contains(joined, "ENTER") {
324			t.Error("QA-GL-11: ENTER to start prompt disappeared in waiting phase")
325		}
326	
327		// playing phase
328		s.PlayerStartsGame()
329		phaseBefore := s.Phase
330		accepted = s.PlayerRestartsGame()
331		if accepted {
332			t.Error("QA-GL-11: restart accepted in playing phase")
333		}
334		if s.Phase != phaseBefore {
335			t.Errorf("QA-GL-11: phase changed from playing on rejected restart, now %v", s.Phase)
336		}
337	}

339	// QA-GL-12: Full lifecycle round-trip
340	func TestQA_GL12_FullLifecycleRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
341		s := qaSession()
342	
343		// Step 1: verify waiting
344		rows := qaFrame(s)
345		joined := qaJoin(rows)
346		if !strings.Contains(joined, "ENTER") {
347			t.Error("QA-GL-12 step 1: ENTER to start not visible in waiting")
348		}
349		if s.Phase != game.PhaseWaiting {
350			t.Errorf("QA-GL-12 step 1: expected waiting, got %v", s.Phase)
351		}
352	
353		// Step 2: start → playing
354		s.PlayerStartsGame()
355		if s.Phase != game.PhasePlaying {
356			t.Errorf("QA-GL-12 step 2: expected playing after start, got %v", s.Phase)
357		}
358		rows = qaFrame(s)
359		joined = qaJoin(rows)
360		if strings.Contains(joined, "ENTER") {
361			t.Error("QA-GL-12 step 2: ENTER hint visible in playing state")
362		}
363		if !hasActivePiece(rows) {
364			t.Error("QA-GL-12 step 2: no active piece visible after start")
365		}
366	
367		// Step 3: force game over
368		for row := -2; row < game.BoardRows; row++ {
369			for col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {
370				s.Board.SetCell(col, row, game.KindI)
371			}
372		}
373		s.SpawnPiece()
374		if s.Phase != game.PhaseGameOver {
375			t.Errorf("QA-GL-12 step 3: expected game_over, got %v", s.Phase)
376		}
377		rows = qaFrame(s)
378		joined = qaJoin(rows)
379		if !strings.Contains(joined, "GAME OVER") {
380			t.Error("QA-GL-12 step 3: GAME OVER overlay not visible")
381		}
382	
383		// Step 4: restart → waiting (then auto-start → playing in current impl)
384		s.PlayerRestartsGame()
385		if s.Phase == game.PhaseGameOver {
386			t.Error("QA-GL-12 step 4: still in game_over after restart")
387		}
388		rows = qaFrame(s)
389		joined = qaJoin(rows)
390		if strings.Contains(joined, "GAME OVER") {
391			t.Error("QA-GL-12 step 4: GAME OVER still visible after restart")
392		}
393	
394		// Step 5: verify new clean playing state (restart auto-starts)
395		if s.Score != 0 || s.Level != 1 || s.LinesClearedTotal != 0 {
396			t.Errorf("QA-GL-12 step 5: stats not reset — score=%d level=%d lines=%d", s.Score, s.Level, s.LinesClearedTotal)
397		}
398	}
399	
400	// --- helpers ---
401	
402	// containsBlockLetterRow returns true if any frame row contains a dense sequence
403	// of block characters that form part of the TETRIS overlay.
404	func containsBlockLetterRow(rows []string) bool {
405		for _, row := range rows {
406			if strings.Count(row, "█") >= 4 {
407				return true
408			}
409		}
410		return false
411	}
412	
413	// hasActivePiece returns true when the rendered frame contains colored block
414	// characters inside the board area — indicating an active piece is visible.
415	func hasActivePiece(rows []string) bool {
416		// The render uses "██" for piece cells.
417		for _, row := range rows {
418			if strings.Contains(row, "██") {
419				return true
420			}
421		}
422		return false
423	}
424	
425	// verifyBagCycle checks that a slice of 7 kinds contains exactly one of each tetromino.
426	func verifyBagCycle(t *testing.T, label string, kinds []game.Kind) {
427		t.Helper()
428		counts := make(map[game.Kind]int)
429		for _, k := range kinds {
430			counts[k]++
431		}
432		for _, k := range game.AllKinds {
433			if counts[k] != 1 {
434				t.Errorf("%s: kind %v appears %d times (want 1)", label, k, counts[k])
435			}
436		}
437	}

  "new_string": "// QA-GL-6: NEXT panel shows the upcoming piece before it spawns\nfunc TestQA_GL6_NextPanelPreviewsCorrectly(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := qaSession()\n\ts.PlayerStartsGame()\n\tif s.NextKindForTest() == game.KindNone {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-GL-6: NextKind is none after start — cannot verify NEXT panel\")\n\t}\n\tpreviewKind := s.NextKindForTest()\n\ts.PlayerHardDrops()\n\tif s.ActivePieceNilForTest() {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-GL-6: no active piece after hard drop\")\n\t}\n\tif s.ActiveKindForTest() != previewKind {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-GL-6: spawned kind = %v, NEXT showed %v — mismatch\", s.ActiveKindForTest(), previewKind)\n\t}\n}"

  "old_string": "// QA-GL-9: Game over — board freezes and overlay appears\nfunc TestQA_GL9_GameOverOverlayAppearsOnBoardFill(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := qaSession()\n\ts.PlayerStartsGame()\n\tfor row := -2; row < game.BoardRows; row++ {\n\t\tfor col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {\n\t\t\ts.Board.SetCell(col, row, game.KindI)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\ts.SpawnPiece()\n\n\tif s.Phase != game.PhaseGameOver {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-GL-9: phase = %v, want game_over after board fill\", s.Phase)\n\t}\n\n\trows := qaFrame(s)\n\tjoined := qaJoin(rows)\n\tif !strings.Contains(joined, \"GAME OVER\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-GL-9: GAME OVER overlay not visible in rendered frame\")\n\t}\n\t// R to restart hint\n\tif !strings.Contains(joined, \"restart\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-GL-9: 'restart' hint not visible in game_over frame\")\n\t}\n\t// Input rejection: none of the movement/hold/pause calls should change phase.\n\ts.PlayerMovesLeft()\n\ts.PlayerMovesRight()\n\ts.PlayerSoftDrops()\n\ts.PlayerHardDrops()\n\ts.PlayerHolds()\n\ts.PlayerPauses()\n\tif s.Phase != game.PhaseGameOver {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-GL-9: phase changed on rejected input during game_over\")\n\t}\n}",
  "new_string": "// QA-GL-9: Game over — board freezes and overlay appears\nfunc TestQA_GL9_GameOverOverlayAppearsOnBoardFill(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := qaSession()\n\ts.PlayerStartsGame()\n\tfor row := -2; row < game.BoardRows; row++ {\n\t\tfor col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {\n\t\t\ts.SetBoardCellForTest(col, row, game.KindI)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\ts.SpawnPiece()\n\n\tif s.CurrentPhase() != game.PhaseGameOver {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-GL-9: phase = %v, want game_over after board fill\", s.CurrentPhase())\n\t}\n\n\trows := qaFrame(s)\n\tjoined := qaJoin(rows)\n\tif !strings.Contains(joined, \"GAME OVER\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-GL-9: GAME OVER overlay not visible in rendered frame\")\n\t}\n\t// R to restart hint\n\tif !strings.Contains(joined, \"restart\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-GL-9: 'restart' hint not visible in game_over frame\")\n\t}\n\t// Input rejection: none of the movement/hold/pause calls should change phase.\n\ts.PlayerMovesLeft()\n\ts.PlayerMovesRight()\n\ts.PlayerSoftDrops()\n\ts.PlayerHardDrops()\n\ts.PlayerHolds()\n\ts.PlayerPauses()\n\tif s.CurrentPhase() != game.PhaseGameOver {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-GL-9: phase changed on rejected input during game_over\")\n\t}\n}"
}

  "old_string": "// QA-GL-10: Restart from game over returns to waiting screen\nfunc TestQA_GL10_RestartFromGameOverReturnsToWaiting(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := qaSession()\n\ts.PlayerStartsGame()\n\tfor row := -2; row < game.BoardRows; row++ {\n\t\tfor col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {\n\t\t\ts.Board.SetCell(col, row, game.KindI)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\ts.SpawnPiece()\n\tif s.Phase != game.PhaseGameOver {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-GL-10: setup did not reach game_over\")\n\t}\n\n\ts.PlayerRestartsGame()\n\n\t// PlayerRestartsGame → waiting → auto-starts → playing; verify intermediate steps\n\t// In the current implementation: restart transitions to waiting then immediately starts.\n\t// The final visible state should be playing (per session.go: PlayerRestartsGame calls PlayerStartsGame).\n\tif s.Phase == game.PhaseGameOver {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-GL-10: still in game_over after restart\")\n\t}\n\n\trows := qaFrame(s)\n\tjoined := qaJoin(rows)\n\tif strings.Contains(joined, \"GAME OVER\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-GL-10: GAME OVER overlay still visible after restart\")\n\t}\n\t// Stats must be reset\n\tif s.Score != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-GL-10: score not reset after restart, got %d\", s.Score)\n\t}\n\tif s.Level != 1 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-GL-10: level not reset after restart, got %d\", s.Level)\n\t}\n\tif s.LinesClearedTotal != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-GL-10: lines not reset after restart, got %d\", s.LinesClearedTotal)\n\t}\n\tif s.HoldUsed {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-GL-10: hold_used not reset after restart\")\n\t}\n\tif s.HeldKind != game.KindNone {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-GL-10: held_kind not reset after restart, got %v\", s.HeldKind)\n\t}\n}",
  "new_string": "// QA-GL-10: Restart from game over returns to waiting screen\nfunc TestQA_GL10_RestartFromGameOverReturnsToWaiting(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := qaSession()\n\ts.PlayerStartsGame()\n\tfor row := -2; row < game.BoardRows; row++ {\n\t\tfor col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {\n\t\t\ts.SetBoardCellForTest(col, row, game.KindI)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\ts.SpawnPiece()\n\tif s.CurrentPhase() != game.PhaseGameOver {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-GL-10: setup did not reach game_over\")\n\t}\n\n\ts.PlayerRestartsGame()\n\n\t// PlayerRestartsGame → waiting → auto-starts → playing; verify intermediate steps\n\t// In the current implementation: restart transitions to waiting then immediately starts.\n\t// The final visible state should be playing (per session.go: PlayerRestartsGame calls PlayerStartsGame).\n\tif s.CurrentPhase() == game.PhaseGameOver {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-GL-10: still in game_over after restart\")\n\t}\n\n\trows := qaFrame(s)\n\tjoined := qaJoin(rows)\n\tif strings.Contains(joined, \"GAME OVER\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-GL-10: GAME OVER overlay still visible after restart\")\n\t}\n\t// Stats must be reset\n\tif s.ScoreForTest() != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-GL-10: score not reset after restart, got %d\", s.ScoreForTest())\n\t}\n\tif s.LevelForTest() != 1 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-GL-10: level not reset after restart, got %d\", s.LevelForTest())\n\t}\n\tif s.LinesClearedTotalForTest() != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-GL-10: lines not reset after restart, got %d\", s.LinesClearedTotalForTest())\n\t}\n\tif s.HoldUsedForTest() {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-GL-10: hold_used not reset after restart\")\n\t}\n\tif s.HeldKindForTest() != game.KindNone {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-GL-10: held_kind not reset after restart, got %v\", s.HeldKindForTest())\n\t}\n}"
}

15	// TestLNSStatZeroPadded verifies lines stat renders as 3-digit zero-padded within 6-wide field.
16	func TestLNSStatZeroPadded(t *testing.T) {
17		s := qaSession()
18		rows := qaFrame(s)
19		joined := qaJoin(rows)
20		// DESIGN.md: lines 3 digits (e.g. "000" at start, right-aligned in 6-wide interior).
21		if !strings.Contains(joined, "   000") {
22			t.Errorf("LNS stat: expected '   000' (3-zero right-aligned), got frame:\n%s", joined)
23		}
24	}
25

34			t.Errorf("LVL stat: expected '    01' (2-zero right-aligned for level 1), got frame:\n%s", joined)
35		}
36	}

52		}
53	}

107	// TestLineClearFlashRendersWhiteOnFlashRows verifies that cleared rows render as ██ in flash
108	// color (#FFFFFF) when RenderState.FlashRows is set. This is a golden-snapshot test against
109	// a hand-built RenderState — no timing dependency.
110	func TestLineClearFlashRendersWhiteOnFlashRows(t *testing.T) {
111		// Build a RenderState with row 19 (bottom row) flagged as a flash row and fully locked.
112		var grid game.CellGrid
113		for col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {
114			grid[19][col] = game.KindI
115		}
116		rs := game.RenderState{
117			Phase:    game.PhasePlaying,
118			Board:    grid,
119			FlashRows: []int{19},
120			Level:    1,
121		}
122		frame := Render(rs, 40, 24)
123		rows := frame.rows
124	
125		// Row 19 in the board is render row 20 (border row 0 + 20 cell rows + border row 21;
126		// board row N maps to assembly row N+1). With topPad = (24-22)/2 = 1, assembly row 20
127		// is output row 21.
128		const boardCellRow = 19
129		assemblyRow := boardCellRow + 1 // +1 for top border
130		topPad := (24 - 22) / 2
131		outputRow := assemblyRow + topPad
132	
133		if outputRow >= len(rows) {
134			t.Fatalf("output row %d out of range (frame has %d rows)", outputRow, len(rows))
135		}
136		flashRow := rows[outputRow]
137	
138		// The flash color is #FFFFFF — both fg and bg. The ANSI sequence contains "255;255;255".
139		if !strings.Contains(flashRow, "255;255;255") {
140			t.Errorf("flash row %d: expected #FFFFFF ANSI color sequence, got: %s", outputRow, stripANSI(flashRow))
141		}
142		// Every cell in the flash row must be "██" (2 block chars per cell, 10 cells).
143		plain := stripANSI(flashRow)
144		blockCount := strings.Count(plain, "██")
145		if blockCount < game.BoardCols {
146			t.Errorf("flash row: expected ≥%d block pairs (██), got %d in: %q", game.BoardCols, blockCount, plain)
147		}
148	}
149	
150	// TestNonFlashRowsUnaffectedByFlashRows verifies that rows NOT in FlashRows render normally
151	// even when FlashRows is non-nil.
152	func TestNonFlashRowsUnaffectedByFlashRows(t *testing.T) {
153		var grid game.CellGrid
154		for col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {
155			grid[19][col] = game.KindI
156			grid[18][col] = game.KindJ
157		}
158		rs := game.RenderState{
159			Phase:    game.PhasePlaying,
160			Board:    grid,
161			FlashRows: []int{19}, // only row 19 flashes; row 18 should render locked
162			Level:    1,
163		}
164		frame := Render(rs, 40, 24)
165		rows := frame.rows
166	
167		const boardCellRow = 18
168		assemblyRow := boardCellRow + 1
169		topPad := (24 - 22) / 2
170		outputRow := assemblyRow + topPad
171	
172		if outputRow >= len(rows) {
173			t.Fatalf("output row %d out of range", outputRow)
174		}
175		nonFlashRow := rows[outputRow]
176	
177		// Row 18 must NOT contain flash color.
178		if strings.Contains(nonFlashRow, "255;255;255") {
179			t.Errorf("non-flash row %d: unexpected flash color (#FFFFFF) present", outputRow)
180		}
181		// Row 18 should contain locked-cell block glyphs (not empty "· ").
182		plain := stripANSI(nonFlashRow)
183		if !strings.Contains(plain, "██") {
184			t.Errorf("non-flash locked row %d: expected ██ locked cells, got: %q", outputRow, plain)
185		}
186	}
187	
188	// TestApplyPrefillFillsBottomRows verifies that applyPrefill fills the correct number of rows.
189	func TestApplyPrefillFillsBottomRows(t *testing.T) {
190		s := qaSession()
191		s.PlayerStartsGame()
192		applyPrefill(s, 2, 9)
193		// Bottom 2 rows (18, 19) must be filled except col 9.
194		for row := 18; row <= 19; row++ {
195			for col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {
196				cell := s.Board.Cell(col, row)
197				if col == 9 {
198					if cell != game.KindNone {
199						t.Errorf("applyPrefill: row %d col 9 (gap col) should be empty, got %v", row, cell)
200					}
201				} else {
202					if cell == game.KindNone {
203						t.Errorf("applyPrefill: row %d col %d should be filled, got empty", row, col)
204					}
205				}
206			}
207		}
208		// Row 17 must remain empty.
209		for col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {
210			if s.Board.Cell(col, 17) != game.KindNone {
211				t.Errorf("applyPrefill: row 17 should remain empty, got filled at col %d", col)
212			}
213		}
214	}
215	
216	// TestApplyInitLinesSetsLevelAndLines verifies applyInitLines sets counters correctly.
217	func TestApplyInitLinesSetsLevelAndLines(t *testing.T) {
218		s := qaSession()

220		applyInitLines(s, 10)

233		}
234	}

191		applyPrefill(s, 2, 9)
192		// Bottom 2 rows (18, 19) must be filled except col 9.
193		for row := 18; row <= 19; row++ {
194			for col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {
195				cell := s.Board.Cell(col, row)
196				if col == 9 {
197					if cell != game.KindNone {
198						t.Errorf("applyPrefill: row %d col 9 (gap col) should be empty, got %v", row, cell)
199					}
200				} else {
201					if cell == game.KindNone {
202						t.Errorf("applyPrefill: row %d col %d should be filled, got empty", row, col)
203					}
204				}
205			}
206		}
207		// Row 17 must remain empty.
208		for col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {
209			if s.Board.Cell(col, 17) != game.KindNone {
210				t.Errorf("applyPrefill: row 17 should remain empty, got filled at col %d", col)
211			}
212		}
213	}
214	
215	// TestApplyInitLinesSetsLevelAndLines verifies applyInitLines sets counters correctly.
216	func TestApplyInitLinesSetsLevelAndLines(t *testing.T) {
217		s := qaSession()
218		s.PlayerStartsGame()
219		applyInitLines(s, 10)
220		if s.LinesClearedTotal != 10 {
221			t.Errorf("applyInitLines(10): LinesClearedTotal = %d, want 10", s.LinesClearedTotal)
222		}
223		if s.Level != 2 {

225		}
226

230		if s2.Level != 1 {
231			t.Errorf("applyInitLines(9): Level = %d, want 1 (below 10-line threshold)", s2.Level)
232		}
233	}
234	
235	// TestApplyPrefillZeroRowsNoOp verifies applyPrefill(0) leaves the board unchanged.
236	func TestApplyPrefillZeroRowsNoOp(t *testing.T) {
237		s := qaSession()
238		s.PlayerStartsGame()
239		applyPrefill(s, 0, 9)

244				}

The `rs.HoldUsed` and `rs.HeldKind` in `render_test.go` are fields on `game.RenderState` (a value type), not directly on Session. These are valid — `RenderState` is a public type owned by `pkg/game` and its fields are part of the public API for rendering. These don't need to change.

1	// QA suite: executable conversion of features/movement_and_rotation_qa.md
2	// Each test corresponds to a QA-MR-N procedure and verifies rendered frame
3	// using the same Render() surface the user observes in the terminal.
4	package tui
5	
6	import (
7		"math/rand"
8		"strings"
9		"testing"
10		"time"
11	
12		"github.com/gamaleds/tetris/pkg/game"
13	)
14	
15	func mrSession() *game.Session {
16		s := game.NewSession(rand.New(rand.NewSource(7)))
17		s.PlayerStartsGame()
18		return s
19	}
20	
21	func mrFrame(s *game.Session) []string {
22		return Render(s.Snapshot(), 40, 24).PlainRows()
23	}
24	
25	// findActivePieceLeftCol returns the leftmost board column occupied by the active piece.
26	func findActivePieceLeftCol(s *game.Session) int {
27		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
28			return -1
29		}
30		fp := s.ActivePiece.Footprint()
31		minCol := fp[0].Col
32		for _, p := range fp {
33			if p.Col < minCol {
34				minCol = p.Col
35			}
36		}
37		return minCol
38	}
39	
40	// findActivePieceRightCol returns the rightmost board column occupied by the active piece.
41	func findActivePieceRightCol(s *game.Session) int {
42		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
43			return -1
44		}
45		fp := s.ActivePiece.Footprint()
46		maxCol := fp[0].Col
47		for _, p := range fp {
48			if p.Col > maxCol {
49				maxCol = p.Col
50			}
51		}
52		return maxCol
53	}
54	
55	// findNonOPiece returns when the active piece is not an O-piece (rotation-symmetric).
56	// It hard-drops until a non-O piece spawns, or fails.
57	func findNonOPiece(t *testing.T, s *game.Session) {
58		t.Helper()
59		for i := 0; i < 14; i++ {
60			if s.ActivePiece == nil {
61				t.Fatal("findNonOPiece: no active piece")
62			}
63			if s.ActivePiece.Kind != game.KindO {
64				return
65			}
66			s.PlayerHardDrops()
67		}
68		t.Skip("findNonOPiece: could not find non-O piece in 14 spawns")
69	}

71	// QA-MR-1: Move Left shifts piece visually
72	func TestQA_MR1_MoveLeftShiftsPiece(t *testing.T) {
73		s := mrSession()
74		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
75			t.Fatal("QA-MR-1: no active piece after start")
76		}
77		// Ensure not at left wall.
78		for findActivePieceLeftCol(s) == 0 {
79			s.PlayerHardDrops()
80			if s.ActivePiece == nil {
81				t.Fatal("QA-MR-1: piece disappeared")
82			}
83		}
84		col0 := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col
85		ok := s.PlayerMovesLeft()
86		if !ok {
87			t.Fatal("QA-MR-1: PlayerMovesLeft returned false (not at wall)")
88		}
89		if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col != col0-1 {
90			t.Errorf("QA-MR-1: origin col = %d, want %d", s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col, col0-1)
91		}
92		rows := mrFrame(s)
93		if !hasActivePiece(rows) {
94			t.Error("QA-MR-1: active piece not visible in rendered frame after move left")
95		}
96		joined := strings.Join(rows, "\n")
97		if !strings.Contains(joined, "░░") {
98			t.Error("QA-MR-1: ghost piece not visible after move left")
99		}
100	}

238	// QA-MR-7: Rotation blocked when all SRS kick positions are occupied
239	func TestQA_MR7_RotationBlockedWhenNoKickSucceeds(t *testing.T) {
240		s := mrSession()
241		// Find a T-piece for reliable testing; it needs 4 positions of wall-kick.
242		for i := 0; i < 14; i++ {
243			if s.ActivePiece != nil && s.ActivePiece.Kind == game.KindT {
244				break
245			}
246			s.PlayerHardDrops()
247		}
248		if s.ActivePiece == nil || s.ActivePiece.Kind != game.KindT {
249			t.Skip("QA-MR-7: T-piece not available in 14 spawns; skip")
250		}
251		// Fill all board cells except the active piece cells to block rotation.
252		fp := s.ActivePiece.Footprint()
253		fpSet := make(map[game.Position]bool)
254		for _, p := range fp {
255			fpSet[p] = true
256		}
257		for r := 0; r < game.BoardRows; r++ {
258			for c := 0; c < game.BoardCols; c++ {
259				pos := game.Position{Row: r, Col: c}
260				if !fpSet[pos] {
261					s.Board.SetCell(c, r, game.KindI)
262				}
263			}
264		}
265		rot0 := s.ActivePiece.Rotation
266		col0 := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col
267		row0 := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row
268		ok := s.PlayerRotatesCW()
269		if ok {
270			// If it succeeded, all kicks may have been tried but one happened to work.
271			// Only fail if position changed: the piece must not have moved to an invalid cell.
272			t.Logf("QA-MR-7: CW rotation succeeded (unexpected) — verifying placement is valid")
273		} else {
274			if s.ActivePiece.Rotation != rot0 {
275				t.Error("QA-MR-7: rotation changed despite blocked return value")
276			}
277			if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col != col0 || s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != row0 {
278				t.Error("QA-MR-7: piece origin changed despite blocked rotation")
279			}
280		}
281		// No visual glitch.
282		rows := mrFrame(s)
283		if !hasActivePiece(rows) {
284			t.Error("QA-MR-7: active piece not visible after blocked rotation attempt")
285		}
286	}

298	// QA-MR-10: Rotation does not auto-repeat — handleKey fires exactly one rotation per call
299	func TestQA_MR10_RotationDoesNotAutoRepeat(t *testing.T) {
300		s := mrSession()
301		findNonOPiece(t, s)
302		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
303			t.Fatal("QA-MR-10: no active piece")
304		}
305		rot0 := s.ActivePiece.Rotation
306		// Simulate pressing X once (as handleKey does).
307		handleKey(s, KeyX, false)
308		rot1 := s.ActivePiece.Rotation
309		if rot1 == rot0 {
310			t.Error("QA-MR-10: rotation did not fire on first KeyX press")
311		}
312		// Calling handleKey again for KeyX = second press, not auto-repeat.
313		// DAS only fires for KeyLeft/KeyRight; rotation keys do not enter DAS.
314		// Verify that handleKey for non-left/right does NOT trigger a startDAS call.
315		// The DAS state is internal to Run(); we verify by checking that repeated
316		// handleKey calls on X do rotate (they should — each call is one explicit press).
317		handleKey(s, KeyX, false)
318		rot2 := s.ActivePiece.Rotation
319		// Each call rotates once, as expected. Two calls = two explicit presses (not DAS).
320		if rot2 == rot1 {
321			t.Logf("QA-MR-10: second handleKey(KeyX) did not rotate (blocked by SRS) — acceptable")
322		}
323		// The DAS loop in tui.go only invokes dasCh for KeyLeft and KeyRight,
324		// so rotation is never auto-repeated regardless of hold duration.
325		// The test verifies no rotation key enters the DAS path.
326	}

328	// QA-MR-11: Move while at rest resets lock countdown
329	func TestQA_MR11_MoveWhileAtRestResetsLockCountdown(t *testing.T) {
330		s := mrSession()
331		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
332			t.Fatal("QA-MR-11: no active piece after start")
333		}
334		// Drop piece to rest position.
335		ghost := s.ActivePiece.GhostOrigin(s.Board)
336		s.ActivePiece.Origin = ghost
337		s.LockTicksRemaining = game.LockTicks
338		// Decrement partway through.
339		for i := 0; i < game.LockTicks-5; i++ {
340			s.Tick()
341			if s.ActivePiece == nil {
342				t.Fatal("QA-MR-11: piece locked before expected")
343			}
344		}
345		ticksBefore := s.LockTicksRemaining
346		// Move left (if space) or verify at left wall.
347		leftCol := findActivePieceLeftCol(s)
348		if leftCol > 0 {
349			ok := s.PlayerMovesLeft()
350			if !ok {
351				t.Fatal("QA-MR-11: PlayerMovesLeft returned false unexpectedly")
352			}
353			// Lock countdown must reset to LockTicks.
354			if s.LockTicksRemaining != game.LockTicks {
355				t.Errorf("QA-MR-11: LockTicksRemaining = %d after successful move, want %d", s.LockTicksRemaining, game.LockTicks)
356			}
357		} else {
358			// At left wall: move is blocked, countdown stays unchanged.
359			ok := s.PlayerMovesLeft()
360			if ok {
361				t.Error("QA-MR-11: unexpected successful left move at wall")
362			}
363			if s.LockTicksRemaining != ticksBefore {
364				t.Errorf("QA-MR-11: LockTicksRemaining changed on blocked move: %d → %d", ticksBefore, s.LockTicksRemaining)
365			}
366		}
367	}

468	// QA-MR-16: Unrecognized keys are silently ignored
469	func TestQA_MR16_UnrecognizedKeysIgnored(t *testing.T) {
470		s := mrSession()
471		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
472			t.Fatal("QA-MR-16: no active piece after start")
473		}

475		row0 := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row
476		rot0 := s.ActivePiece.Rotation
477		phase0 := s.Phase
478	
479		// Send unknown keys via handleKey (as terminal dispatch does).
480		handleKey(s, KeyUnknown, false)
481		handleKey(s, KeyC, false) // C = hold — not a movement/rotation key
482		// Undo any hold side-effect; only check movement.
483		// Re-read col/row/rot since hold can swap piece.
484		colAfterC := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col
485		rowAfterC := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row
486		_ = colAfterC
487		_ = rowAfterC

489		// Unknown key must not change anything.
490		s2 := mrSession()
491		if s2.ActivePiece == nil {
492			t.Fatal("QA-MR-16: s2 no active piece")

505			t.Errorf("QA-MR-16: phase changed on unknown key: %v→%v", phase0, s2.Phase)
506		}
507		_ = col0
508		_ = row0

  "old_string": "// QA-MR-10: Rotation does not auto-repeat — handleKey fires exactly one rotation per call\nfunc TestQA_MR10_RotationDoesNotAutoRepeat(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := mrSession()\n\tfindNonOPiece(t, s)\n\tif s.ActivePiece == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-MR-10: no active piece\")\n\t}\n\trot0 := s.ActivePiece.Rotation\n\t// Simulate pressing X once (as handleKey does).\n\thandleKey(s, KeyX, false)\n\trot1 := s.ActivePiece.Rotation\n\tif rot1 == rot0 {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-MR-10: rotation did not fire on first KeyX press\")\n\t}\n\t// Calling handleKey again for KeyX = second press, not auto-repeat.\n\t// DAS only fires for KeyLeft/KeyRight; rotation keys do not enter DAS.\n\t// Verify that handleKey for non-left/right does NOT trigger a startDAS call.\n\t// The DAS state is internal to Run(); we verify by checking that repeated\n\t// handleKey calls on X do rotate (they should — each call is one explicit press).\n\thandleKey(s, KeyX, false)\n\trot2 := s.ActivePiece.Rotation\n\t// Each call rotates once, as expected. Two calls = two explicit presses (not DAS).\n\tif rot2 == rot1 {\n\t\tt.Logf(\"QA-MR-10: second handleKey(KeyX) did not rotate (blocked by SRS) — acceptable\")\n\t}\n\t// The DAS loop in tui.go only invokes dasCh for KeyLeft and KeyRight,\n\t// so rotation is never auto-repeated regardless of hold duration.\n\t// The test verifies no rotation key enters the DAS path.\n}",
  "new_string": "// QA-MR-10: Rotation does not auto-repeat — handleKey fires exactly one rotation per call\nfunc TestQA_MR10_RotationDoesNotAutoRepeat(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := mrSession()\n\tfindNonOPiece(t, s)\n\tif s.ActivePieceNilForTest() {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-MR-10: no active piece\")\n\t}\n\trot0 := s.ActiveRotationForTest()\n\t// Simulate pressing X once (as handleKey does).\n\thandleKey(s, KeyX, false)\n\trot1 := s.ActiveRotationForTest()\n\tif rot1 == rot0 {\n\t\tt.Error(\"QA-MR-10: rotation did not fire on first KeyX press\")\n\t}\n\t// Calling handleKey again for KeyX = second press, not auto-repeat.\n\t// DAS only fires for KeyLeft/KeyRight; rotation keys do not enter DAS.\n\t// Verify that handleKey for non-left/right does NOT trigger a startDAS call.\n\t// The DAS state is internal to Run(); we verify by checking that repeated\n\t// handleKey calls on X do rotate (they should — each call is one explicit press).\n\thandleKey(s, KeyX, false)\n\trot2 := s.ActiveRotationForTest()\n\t// Each call rotates once, as expected. Two calls = two explicit presses (not DAS).\n\tif rot2 == rot1 {\n\t\tt.Logf(\"QA-MR-10: second handleKey(KeyX) did not rotate (blocked by SRS) — acceptable\")\n\t}\n\t// The DAS loop in tui.go only invokes dasCh for KeyLeft and KeyRight,\n\t// so rotation is never auto-repeated regardless of hold duration.\n\t// The test verifies no rotation key enters the DAS path.\n}"
}

  "old_string": "// QA-MR-11: Move while at rest resets lock countdown\nfunc TestQA_MR11_MoveWhileAtRestResetsLockCountdown(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := mrSession()\n\tif s.ActivePiece == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-MR-11: no active piece after start\")\n\t}\n\t// Drop piece to rest position.\n\tghost := s.ActivePiece.GhostOrigin(s.Board)\n\ts.ActivePiece.Origin = ghost\n\ts.LockTicksRemaining = game.LockTicks\n\t// Decrement partway through.\n\tfor i := 0; i < game.LockTicks-5; i++ {\n\t\ts.Tick()\n\t\tif s.ActivePiece == nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-MR-11: piece locked before expected\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tticksBefore := s.LockTicksRemaining\n\t// Move left (if space) or verify at left wall.\n\tleftCol := findActivePieceLeftCol(s)\n\tif leftCol > 0 {\n\t\tok := s.PlayerMovesLeft()\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-MR-11: PlayerMovesLeft returned false unexpectedly\")\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Lock countdown must reset to LockTicks.\n\t\tif s.LockTicksRemaining != game.LockTicks {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-MR-11: LockTicksRemaining = %d after successful move, want %d\", s.LockTicksRemaining, game.LockTicks)\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\t// At left wall: move is blocked, countdown stays unchanged.\n\t\tok := s.PlayerMovesLeft()\n\t\tif ok {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"QA-MR-11: unexpected successful left move at wall\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tif s.LockTicksRemaining != ticksBefore {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-MR-11: LockTicksRemaining changed on blocked move: %d → %d\", ticksBefore, s.LockTicksRemaining)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}",
  "new_string": "// QA-MR-11: Move while at rest resets lock countdown\nfunc TestQA_MR11_MoveWhileAtRestResetsLockCountdown(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := mrSession()\n\tif s.ActivePieceNilForTest() {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-MR-11: no active piece after start\")\n\t}\n\t// Drop piece to rest position.\n\ts.SetActivePieceToGhostForTest()\n\ts.SetLockTicksRemainingForTest(game.LockTicks)\n\t// Decrement partway through.\n\tfor i := 0; i < game.LockTicks-5; i++ {\n\t\ts.Tick()\n\t\tif s.ActivePieceNilForTest() {\n\t\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-MR-11: piece locked before expected\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tticksBefore := s.LockTicksRemainingForTest()\n\t// Move left (if space) or verify at left wall.\n\tleftCol := findActivePieceLeftCol(s)\n\tif leftCol > 0 {\n\t\tok := s.PlayerMovesLeft()\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-MR-11: PlayerMovesLeft returned false unexpectedly\")\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Lock countdown must reset to LockTicks.\n\t\tif s.LockTicksRemainingForTest() != game.LockTicks {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-MR-11: LockTicksRemaining = %d after successful move, want %d\", s.LockTicksRemainingForTest(), game.LockTicks)\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\t// At left wall: move is blocked, countdown stays unchanged.\n\t\tok := s.PlayerMovesLeft()\n\t\tif ok {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"QA-MR-11: unexpected successful left move at wall\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tif s.LockTicksRemainingForTest() != ticksBefore {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-MR-11: LockTicksRemaining changed on blocked move: %d → %d\", ticksBefore, s.LockTicksRemainingForTest())\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}"
}

  "old_string": "// QA-MR-16: Unrecognized keys are silently ignored\nfunc TestQA_MR16_UnrecognizedKeysIgnored(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := mrSession()\n\tif s.ActivePiece == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-MR-16: no active piece after start\")\n\t}\n\tcol0 := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col\n\trow0 := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row\n\trot0 := s.ActivePiece.Rotation\n\tphase0 := s.Phase\n\n\t// Send unknown keys via handleKey (as terminal dispatch does).\n\thandleKey(s, KeyUnknown, false)\n\thandleKey(s, KeyC, false) // C = hold — not a movement/rotation key\n\t// Undo any hold side-effect; only check movement.\n\t// Re-read col/row/rot since hold can swap piece.\n\tcolAfterC := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col\n\trowAfterC := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row\n\t_ = colAfterC\n\t_ = rowAfterC\n\n\t// Unknown key must not change anything.\n\ts2 := mrSession()\n\tif s2.ActivePiece == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-MR-16: s2 no active piece\")\n\t}\n\tcol2 := s2.ActivePiece.Origin.Col\n\trow2 := s2.ActivePiece.Origin.Row\n\trot2 := s2.ActivePiece.Rotation\n\thandleKey(s2, KeyUnknown, false)\n\tif s2.ActivePiece.Origin.Col != col2 || s2.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != row2 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-MR-16: piece moved on unknown key: (%d,%d)→(%d,%d)\", col2, row2, s2.ActivePiece.Origin.Col, s2.ActivePiece.Origin.Row)\n\t}\n\tif s2.ActivePiece.Rotation != rot2 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-MR-16: rotation changed on unknown key: %v→%v\", rot2, s2.ActivePiece.Rotation)\n\t}\n\tif s2.Phase != phase0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-MR-16: phase changed on unknown key: %v→%v\", phase0, s2.Phase)\n\t}\n\t_ = col0\n\t_ = row0\n\t_ = rot0\n}",
  "new_string": "// QA-MR-16: Unrecognized keys are silently ignored\nfunc TestQA_MR16_UnrecognizedKeysIgnored(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := mrSession()\n\tif s.ActivePieceNilForTest() {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-MR-16: no active piece after start\")\n\t}\n\tcol0 := s.ActiveOriginForTest().Col\n\trow0 := s.ActiveOriginForTest().Row\n\trot0 := s.ActiveRotationForTest()\n\tphase0 := s.CurrentPhase()\n\n\t// Send unknown keys via handleKey (as terminal dispatch does).\n\thandleKey(s, KeyUnknown, false)\n\thandleKey(s, KeyC, false) // C = hold — not a movement/rotation key\n\t// Undo any hold side-effect; only check movement.\n\t// Re-read col/row/rot since hold can swap piece.\n\tcolAfterC := s.ActiveOriginForTest().Col\n\trowAfterC := s.ActiveOriginForTest().Row\n\t_ = colAfterC\n\t_ = rowAfterC\n\n\t// Unknown key must not change anything.\n\ts2 := mrSession()\n\tif s2.ActivePieceNilForTest() {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-MR-16: s2 no active piece\")\n\t}\n\tcol2 := s2.ActiveOriginForTest().Col\n\trow2 := s2.ActiveOriginForTest().Row\n\trot2 := s2.ActiveRotationForTest()\n\thandleKey(s2, KeyUnknown, false)\n\tif s2.ActiveOriginForTest().Col != col2 || s2.ActiveOriginForTest().Row != row2 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-MR-16: piece moved on unknown key: (%d,%d)→(%d,%d)\", col2, row2, s2.ActiveOriginForTest().Col, s2.ActiveOriginForTest().Row)\n\t}\n\tif s2.ActiveRotationForTest() != rot2 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-MR-16: rotation changed on unknown key: %v→%v\", rot2, s2.ActiveRotationForTest())\n\t}\n\tif s2.CurrentPhase() != phase0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-MR-16: phase changed on unknown key: %v→%v\", phase0, s2.CurrentPhase())\n\t}\n\t_ = col0\n\t_ = row0\n\t_ = rot0\n}"

1	// QA suite: executable conversion of features/gravity_and_lock_qa.md
2	// Each test corresponds to a QA-GL-N procedure and verifies the rendered frame
3	// using the same Render() surface the user observes in the terminal.
4	package tui
5	
6	import (
7		"math/rand"
8		"strings"
9		"testing"
10	
11		"github.com/gamaleds/tetris/pkg/game"
12	)
13	
14	func glSession() *game.Session {
15		s := game.NewSession(rand.New(rand.NewSource(1)))
16		s.PlayerStartsGame()
17		return s
18	}
19	
20	func glFrame(s *game.Session) []string {
21		return Render(s.Snapshot(), 40, 24).PlainRows()
22	}
23	
24	func glJoin(rows []string) string {
25		return strings.Join(rows, "\n")
26	}
27	
28	// countBlockRows counts frame rows containing "██" (active or locked cells).
29	func countBlockRows(rows []string) int {
30		n := 0
31		for _, r := range rows {
32			if strings.Contains(r, "██") {
33				n++
34			}
35		}
36		return n
37	}
38	
39	// QA-GL-1: Active piece descends one row per tick
40	// Verifies that the rendered frame shows the piece one row lower after each Tick().
41	func TestQAGL_GL1_PieceDescendsOneRowPerTick(t *testing.T) {
42		s := glSession()
43		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
44			t.Fatal("QA-GL-1: no active piece after start")
45		}
46		row0 := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row
47		s.Tick()
48		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
49			t.Fatal("QA-GL-1: active piece nil after first tick")
50		}
51		row1 := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row
52		if row1 != row0+1 {
53			t.Errorf("QA-GL-1: after tick 1: row = %d, want %d", row1, row0+1)
54		}
55		s.Tick()
56		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
57			t.Fatal("QA-GL-1: active piece nil after second tick")
58		}
59		row2 := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row
60		if row2 != row0+2 {
61			t.Errorf("QA-GL-1: after tick 2: row = %d, want %d", row2, row0+2)
62		}
63		// Render surface: active piece must be visible in frame.
64		rows := glFrame(s)
65		if !hasActivePiece(rows) {
66			t.Error("QA-GL-1: no active piece visible in rendered frame after ticks")
67		}
68	}
69	
70	// QA-GL-2: Piece stops descending when at rest
71	// Verifies that once at rest, ticks do not move the piece further down.
72	func TestQAGL_GL2_PieceStopsWhenAtRest(t *testing.T) {
73		s := glSession()
74		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
75			t.Fatal("QA-GL-2: no active piece after start")
76		}
77		ghost := s.ActivePiece.GhostOrigin(s.Board)
78		s.ActivePiece.Origin = ghost
79		s.LockTicksRemaining = game.LockTicks
80	
81		restRow := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row
82		// Tick: piece is at rest, so it should not move down.
83		s.Tick()
84		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
85			// A lock occurred — means lock_ticks decremented to 0. That's unexpected with LockTicks=30.
86			t.Fatal("QA-GL-2: piece locked unexpectedly on first tick at rest with LockTicks remaining")
87		}
88		if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != restRow {
89			t.Errorf("QA-GL-2: piece moved while at rest: row = %d, want %d", s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row, restRow)
90		}
91		// Rendered frame must show the piece as active, not locked.
92		rows := glFrame(s)
93		if !hasActivePiece(rows) {
94			t.Error("QA-GL-2: no active piece visible while at rest")
95		}
96	}
97	
98	// QA-GL-3: Ghost piece shows landing position
99	// Verifies rendered frame contains ghost cells (░░) while piece is above floor.
100	func TestQAGL_GL3_GhostPieceVisible(t *testing.T) {
101		s := glSession()
102		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
103			t.Fatal("QA-GL-3: no active piece after start")
104		}
105		ghost := s.ActivePiece.GhostOrigin(s.Board)
106		// Ghost is only distinct when piece is not already at ghost position.
107		if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row == ghost.Row {
108			// Move piece up so ghost differs — use a mid-board position.
109			s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row = 0
110		}
111		rows := glFrame(s)
112		joined := glJoin(rows)
113		if !strings.Contains(joined, "░░") {
114			t.Error("QA-GL-3: ghost cells (░░) not visible in rendered frame")
115		}
116	}
117	
118	// QA-GL-4: Piece locks after lock countdown expires
119	// Verifies that after LockTicks ticks while at rest, the piece locks and a new one spawns.
120	func TestQAGL_GL4_PieceLocksAfterCountdown(t *testing.T) {
121		s := glSession()
122		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
123			t.Fatal("QA-GL-4: no active piece after start")
124		}
125		ghost := s.ActivePiece.GhostOrigin(s.Board)
126		s.ActivePiece.Origin = ghost
127		s.LockTicksRemaining = game.LockTicks
128	
129		var lockedKind game.Kind
130		if s.ActivePiece != nil {
131			lockedKind = s.ActivePiece.Kind
132		}
133	
134		// Tick LockTicks times; on the last tick, piece must lock.
135		for i := 0; i < game.LockTicks; i++ {
136			if s.ActivePiece == nil {
137				t.Fatalf("QA-GL-4: active piece nil at tick %d (too early lock)", i+1)
138			}
139			s.Tick()
140		}
141		// After LockTicks ticks, piece should have locked and a new piece spawned.
142		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
143			t.Fatal("QA-GL-4: active piece nil after lock — expected new piece to spawn")
144		}
145		// New piece must be at spawn row.
146		if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != game.SpawnRow {
147			t.Errorf("QA-GL-4: new piece row = %d, want %d", s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row, game.SpawnRow)
148		}
149		// Render: locked cells must appear in board (the locked kind should be visible).
150		rs := s.Snapshot()
151		found := false
152		for r := 0; r < game.BoardRows; r++ {
153			for c := 0; c < game.BoardCols; c++ {
154				if rs.Board[r][c] == lockedKind {
155					found = true
156				}
157			}
158		}
159		if !found {
160			t.Error("QA-GL-4: locked piece cells not written to board after countdown")
161		}
162	}
163	
164	// QA-GL-5: Locked piece footprint appears on the board
165	// Verifies that exactly the footprint cells of the locked piece appear on the board.
166	func TestQAGL_GL5_LockedFootprintOnBoard(t *testing.T) {
167		s := glSession()
168		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
169			t.Fatal("QA-GL-5: no active piece after start")
170		}
171		ghost := s.ActivePiece.GhostOrigin(s.Board)
172		s.ActivePiece.Origin = ghost
173		expectedFootprint := s.ActivePiece.Footprint()
174		expectedKind := s.ActivePiece.Kind
175		s.LockTicksRemaining = 1
176		s.Tick() // triggers lock
177	
178		rs := s.Snapshot()
179		for _, p := range expectedFootprint {
180			if p.Row < 0 || p.Row >= game.BoardRows {
181				continue
182			}
183			if rs.Board[p.Row][p.Col] != expectedKind {
184				t.Errorf("QA-GL-5: board[%d][%d] = %v, want %v (locked footprint)", p.Row, p.Col, rs.Board[p.Row][p.Col], expectedKind)
185			}
186		}
187		// Verify no unexpected cells of that kind outside the footprint (within visible rows).
188		fpSet := make(map[game.Position]bool, len(expectedFootprint))
189		for _, p := range expectedFootprint {
190			fpSet[p] = true
191		}
192		// Check frame has locked cells rendered (██).
193		rows := glFrame(s)
194		joined := glJoin(rows)
195		if !strings.Contains(joined, "██") {
196			t.Error("QA-GL-5: no locked cells (██) visible in rendered frame after lock")
197		}
198	}

200	// QA-GL-6: Hard drop instantly snaps piece to landing position
201	// Verifies that pressing Space locks the piece at ghost position and spawns new piece.
202	func TestQAGL_GL6_HardDropSnapsToGhost(t *testing.T) {
203		s := glSession()
204		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
205			t.Fatal("QA-GL-6: no active piece after start")
206		}
207		ghost := s.ActivePiece.GhostOrigin(s.Board)
208		expectedKind := s.ActivePiece.Kind
209		expectedFP := s.ActivePiece.Footprint()
210		_ = expectedFP
211	
212		// Simulate Space key via PlayerHardDrops (same path as handleKey KeySpace).
213		s.PlayerHardDrops()
214	
215		// New piece must have spawned.
216		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
217			t.Fatal("QA-GL-6: no new piece spawned after hard drop")
218		}
219		if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != game.SpawnRow {
220			t.Errorf("QA-GL-6: new piece row = %d, want %d", s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row, game.SpawnRow)
221		}
222		// Locked kind must appear on the board somewhere (cells are at ghost.Row + offsets).
223		rs := s.Snapshot()
224		foundLocked := false
225		for r := 0; r < game.BoardRows; r++ {
226			for c := 0; c < game.BoardCols; c++ {
227				if rs.Board[r][c] == expectedKind {
228					foundLocked = true
229				}
230			}
231		}
232		if !foundLocked {
233			t.Errorf("QA-GL-6: no locked cell of kind %v on board after hard drop (ghost origin row=%d)", expectedKind, ghost.Row)
234		}
235	}

283	// QA-GL-9: Ticks do not fire while paused
284	// Verifies that calling Tick() in PausedPhase does not move the piece.
285	func TestQAGL_GL9_TicksIgnoredWhilePaused(t *testing.T) {
286		s := glSession()
287		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
288			t.Fatal("QA-GL-9: no active piece after start")
289		}
290		s.PlayerPauses()
291		pieceRow := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row
292		// Simulate 6 ticks that would have fired during pause.
293		for i := 0; i < 6; i++ {
294			s.Tick()
295		}
296		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
297			t.Fatal("QA-GL-9: active piece nil during pause")
298		}
299		if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != pieceRow {
300			t.Errorf("QA-GL-9: piece moved during pause: row = %d, want %d", s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row, pieceRow)
301		}

303		rows := glFrame(s)

306			t.Error("QA-GL-9: paused fill not visible in rendered frame during pause")
307		}

309		s.PlayerResumes()
310		if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != pieceRow {
311			t.Errorf("QA-GL-9: piece at wrong row after resume: got %d, want %d", s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row, pieceRow)
312		}
313	}

345	// QA-GL-11: Full gravity-to-lock-to-spawn cycle runs autonomously
346	// Verifies three complete gravity → rest → lock → spawn cycles without input.
347	func REDACTED(t *testing.T) {
348		s := glSession()
349		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
350			t.Fatal("QA-GL-11: no active piece after start")
351		}
352	
353		for cycle := 1; cycle <= 3; cycle++ {
354			if s.Phase != game.PhasePlaying {
355				t.Fatalf("QA-GL-11 cycle %d: phase = %v, want playing", cycle, s.Phase)
356			}
357			if s.ActivePiece == nil {
358				t.Fatalf("QA-GL-11 cycle %d: no active piece at start of cycle", cycle)
359			}
360			// Tick until at rest.
361			for s.ActivePiece != nil && !s.ActivePiece.AtRest(s.Board) {
362				s.Tick()
363			}
364			if s.ActivePiece == nil {
365				// Spawned during descent (e.g. line clear). A new piece is fine — still counts.
366				if s.Phase != game.PhasePlaying {
367					t.Fatalf("QA-GL-11 cycle %d: game ended unexpectedly at rest check", cycle)
368				}
369				continue
370			}
371			// Piece is at rest. Tick LockTicks times.
372			s.LockTicksRemaining = game.LockTicks
373			for i := 0; i < game.LockTicks; i++ {
374				if s.ActivePiece == nil {
375					t.Fatalf("QA-GL-11 cycle %d: piece locked at tick %d (before countdown expired)", cycle, i+1)
376				}
377				s.Tick()
378			}
379			// After countdown: piece must have locked and new piece spawned.
380			if s.Phase == game.PhaseGameOver {
381				t.Logf("QA-GL-11 cycle %d: game over — board full; cycle passes (lock+spawn happened)", cycle)
382				return
383			}
384			if s.ActivePiece == nil {
385				t.Fatalf("QA-GL-11 cycle %d: no new piece after lock countdown", cycle)
386			}
387			// New piece must be at spawn row.
388			if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != game.SpawnRow {
389				t.Errorf("QA-GL-11 cycle %d: new piece row = %d, want %d", cycle, s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row, game.SpawnRow)
390			}
391			// Render: frame shows active piece.
392			rows := glFrame(s)
393			if !hasActivePiece(rows) {
394				t.Errorf("QA-GL-11 cycle %d: no active piece visible in rendered frame after spawn", cycle)
395			}
396		}
397	}
398

> AGENT

This is a large file. I'll migrate all direct field accesses systematically. The key patterns are:
- `s.ActivePiece == nil` → `s.ActivePieceNilForTest()`
- `s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row` → `s.ActiveOriginForTest().Row`
- `s.ActivePiece.GhostOrigin(s.Board)` → `s.ActiveGhostOriginForTest()`
- `s.ActivePiece.Origin = ghost` → `s.SetActivePieceToGhostForTest()` (when ghost = GhostOrigin)
- `s.LockTicksRemaining = ...` → `s.SetLockTicksRemainingForTest(...)`
- `s.Score` → `s.ScoreForTest()`
- `s.Phase` → `s.CurrentPhase()`
- `s.ActivePiece.AtRest(s.Board)` → `s.ActiveAtRestForTest()`
- `s.ActivePiece.Footprint()` → `s.ActiveFootprintForTest()`
- `s.ActivePiece.Kind` → `s.ActiveKindForTest()`
- `s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row = 0` → need `SetActivePosForTest`

Note: For the ghost case in GL-3 where it sets `s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row = 0` arbitrarily, I can use `s.SetActivePosForTest(col, 0)`. Let me write the replacement:

  "new_string": "// QA-GL-6: Hard drop instantly snaps piece to landing position\n// Verifies that pressing Space locks the piece at ghost position and spawns new piece.\nfunc TestQAGL_GL6_HardDropSnapsToGhost(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := glSession()\n\tif s.ActivePieceNilForTest() {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-GL-6: no active piece after start\")\n\t}\n\tghost := s.ActiveGhostOriginForTest()\n\texpectedKind := s.ActiveKindForTest()\n\n\t// Simulate Space key via PlayerHardDrops (same path as handleKey KeySpace).\n\ts.PlayerHardDrops()\n\n\t// New piece must have spawned.\n\tif s.ActivePieceNilForTest() {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"QA-GL-6: no new piece spawned after hard drop\")\n\t}\n\tif s.ActiveOriginForTest().Row != game.SpawnRow {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"QA-GL-6: new piece row = %d, want %d\", s.ActiveOriginForTest().Row, game.SpawnRow)\n\t}"

1	// QA suite: executable conversion of features/soft_drop_qa.md
2	// Each test corresponds to a QA-SD-N procedure and verifies rendered frame
3	// using the same Render() surface the user observes in the terminal.
4	package tui
5	
6	import (
7		"math/rand"
8		"strings"
9		"testing"
10	
11		"github.com/gamaleds/tetris/pkg/game"
12	)
13	
14	func sdSession() *game.Session {
15		s := game.NewSession(rand.New(rand.NewSource(42)))
16		s.PlayerStartsGame()
17		return s
18	}
19	
20	func sdFrame(s *game.Session) []string {
21		return Render(s.Snapshot(), 40, 24).PlainRows()
22	}
23	
24	// sdJoin joins frame rows for substring matching.
25	func sdJoin(rows []string) string {
26		return strings.Join(rows, "\n")
27	}
28	
29	// sdPieceNotAtRest places an O-piece at the given row (not at rest on empty board).
30	func sdPieceNotAtRest(t *testing.T, s *game.Session, row int) {
31		t.Helper()
32		s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{
33			Kind:     game.KindO,
34			Origin:   game.Position{Col: 4, Row: row},
35			Rotation: game.RotationNorth,
36		}
37		if s.ActivePiece.AtRest(s.Board) {
38			t.Errorf("setup: O-piece at row %d is already at rest — adjust test row", row)
39		}
40	}
41	
42	// sdActivePieceRows returns the set of board rows occupied by the active piece footprint.
43	func sdActivePieceRows(s *game.Session) map[int]bool {
44		rows := map[int]bool{}
45		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
46			return rows
47		}
48		for _, p := range s.ActivePiece.Footprint() {
49			rows[p.Row] = true
50		}
51		return rows
52	}
53	
54	// sdGhostRows returns the set of board rows occupied by the ghost footprint.
55	func sdGhostRows(s *game.Session) map[int]bool {
56		rows := map[int]bool{}
57		if s.ActivePiece == nil {
58			return rows
59		}
60		rs := s.Snapshot()
61		if rs.Active == nil {
62			return rows
63		}
64		for _, p := range rs.Active.Ghost {
65			rows[p.Row] = true
66		}
67		return rows
68	}
69	
70	// minRow returns the minimum value in a non-empty row set.
71	func minRow(rows map[int]bool) int {
72		min := 1<<31 - 1
73		for r := range rows {
74			if r < min {
75				min = r
76			}
77		}
78		return min
79	}
80	
81	// QA-SD-1: Soft drop steps the piece one row down
82	func TestQA_SD1_SoftDropStepsOneRowDown(t *testing.T) {
83		s := sdSession()
84		sdPieceNotAtRest(t, s, 5)
85		if t.Failed() {
86			return
87		}
88	
89		beforeRows := sdActivePieceRows(s)
90		beforeMin := minRow(beforeRows)
91	
92		ok := s.PlayerSoftDrops()
93		if !ok {
94			t.Fatal("QA-SD-1: PlayerSoftDrops returned false (expected accepted)")
95		}
96	
97		afterRows := sdActivePieceRows(s)
98		afterMin := minRow(afterRows)
99		if afterMin != beforeMin+1 {
100			t.Errorf("QA-SD-1: active piece top row: before=%d after=%d, want +1", beforeMin, afterMin)
101		}
102	
103		// Frame must still have structural integrity: board borders and stat panels present.
104		rows := sdFrame(s)
105		joined := sdJoin(rows)
106		for _, want := range []string{"┌", "└", "HOLD", "NEXT", "SCO"} {
107			if !strings.Contains(joined, want) {
108				t.Errorf("QA-SD-1: frame missing %q after soft drop", want)
109			}
110		}
111	}
112	
113	// QA-SD-2: Soft drop awards one point per row
114	func TestQA_SD2_SoftDropAwardsOnePoint(t *testing.T) {
115		s := sdSession()
116		sdPieceNotAtRest(t, s, 5)
117		if t.Failed() {
118			return
119		}
120		s.Score = 40
121	
122		ok := s.PlayerSoftDrops()
123		if !ok {
124			t.Fatal("QA-SD-2: PlayerSoftDrops returned false")
125		}
126		if s.Score != 41 {
127			t.Errorf("QA-SD-2: score = %d, want 41", s.Score)
128		}
129	
130		// Score block must render the updated value.
131		rows := sdFrame(s)
132		joined := sdJoin(rows)
133		if !strings.Contains(joined, "000041") {
134			t.Errorf("QA-SD-2: score block does not show '000041' after soft drop, frame:\n%s", joined)
135		}
136	}

138	// QA-SD-3: Repeated soft drops accumulate one point each
139	func TestQA_SD3_RepeatedSoftDropsAccumulatePoints(t *testing.T) {
140		s := sdSession()
141		sdPieceNotAtRest(t, s, 0)
142		if t.Failed() {
143			return
144		}
145		s.Score = 0
146	
147		accepted := 0
148		for i := 0; i < 5; i++ {
149			if s.PlayerSoftDrops() {
150				accepted++
151			}
152		}
153		if accepted == 0 {
154			t.Fatal("QA-SD-3: no soft drops were accepted")
155		}
156		if s.Score != accepted {
157			t.Errorf("QA-SD-3: score = %d, want %d (one point per accepted drop)", s.Score, accepted)
158		}
159	}

161	// QA-SD-4: Soft drop is blocked when the piece is at rest
162	func TestQA_SD4_SoftDropBlockedAtRest(t *testing.T) {
163		s := sdSession()
164		// Place O-piece at rest position (floor).
165		ap := &game.ActivePiece{
166			Kind:     game.KindO,
167			Origin:   game.Position{Col: 4, Row: 0},
168			Rotation: game.RotationNorth,
169		}
170		ghost := ap.GhostOrigin(s.Board)
171		s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{
172			Kind:     game.KindO,
173			Origin:   ghost,
174			Rotation: game.RotationNorth,
175		}
176		if !s.ActivePiece.AtRest(s.Board) {
177			t.Fatal("QA-SD-4: setup failed — piece is not at rest")
178		}
179		s.Score = 7
180		restRow := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row
181	
182		ok := s.PlayerSoftDrops()
183		if ok {
184			t.Error("QA-SD-4: PlayerSoftDrops returned true when piece is at rest")
185		}
186		if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != restRow {
187			t.Errorf("QA-SD-4: piece moved while at rest: row = %d, want %d", s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row, restRow)
188		}
189		if s.Score != 7 {
190			t.Errorf("QA-SD-4: score changed while blocked: score = %d, want 7", s.Score)
191		}
192	
193		// Frame must show no visual glitch — structural invariants hold.
194		rows := sdFrame(s)
195		joined := sdJoin(rows)
196		if !strings.Contains(joined, "┌") || !strings.Contains(joined, "└") {
197			t.Error("QA-SD-4: board border missing after blocked soft drop")
198		}
199	}

201	// QA-SD-5: Soft drop moves exactly one row, not to the floor
202	func TestQA_SD5_SoftDropOneRowNotSnap(t *testing.T) {
203		s := sdSession()
204		// Place piece high enough that one drop cannot reach the floor.
205		sdPieceNotAtRest(t, s, 0)
206		if t.Failed() {
207			return
208		}
209	
210		ghostR := sdGhostRows(s)
211		floorMin := minRow(ghostR)
212	
213		ok := s.PlayerSoftDrops()
214		if !ok {
215			t.Fatal("QA-SD-5: PlayerSoftDrops returned false")
216		}
217

219		afterMin := minRow(afterRows)
220	
221		// Must not have snapped to ghost/floor.
222		if afterMin >= floorMin {
223			t.Errorf("QA-SD-5: piece snapped to floor (row %d >= ghost floor %d) — expected single-row step", afterMin, floorMin)
224		}

227	// QA-SD-6: Soft drop is ignored when paused
228	func TestQA_SD6_SoftDropIgnoredWhenPaused(t *testing.T) {

236

258	// QA-SD-7: Soft drop is ignored on waiting and game-over screens
259	func TestQA_SD7_SoftDropIgnoredOnWaitingAndGameOver(t *testing.T) {
260		// Waiting phase.
261		sw := game.NewSession(rand.New(rand.NewSource(42)))
262		sw.Score = 0
263		ok := sw.PlayerSoftDrops()
264		if ok {
265			t.Error("QA-SD-7 waiting: PlayerSoftDrops returned true in waiting phase")
266		}
267		if sw.Score != 0 {
268			t.Error("QA-SD-7 waiting: score changed in waiting phase")
269		}
270		rowsW := sdFrame(sw)
271		joinedW := sdJoin(rowsW)
272		if !strings.Contains(joinedW, "ENTER") {
273			t.Error("QA-SD-7 waiting: ENTER hint missing after ignored soft drop")
274		}
275	
276		// Game-over phase.
277		sg := game.NewSession(rand.New(rand.NewSource(42)))
278		sg.PlayerStartsGame()
279		for row := -2; row < game.BoardRows; row++ {
280			for col := 0; col < game.BoardCols; col++ {
281				sg.Board.SetCell(col, row, game.KindI)
282			}
283		}
284		sg.SpawnPiece()
285		if sg.Phase != game.PhaseGameOver {
286			t.Fatal("QA-SD-7 game-over: setup failed to reach game_over")
287		}
288		sg.Score = 0
289		ok = sg.PlayerSoftDrops()
290		if ok {
291			t.Error("QA-SD-7 game-over: PlayerSoftDrops returned true in game_over phase")
292		}
293		if sg.Score != 0 {
294			t.Error("QA-SD-7 game-over: score changed in game_over phase")
295		}
296		rowsGO := sdFrame(sg)
297		joinedGO := sdJoin(rowsGO)
298		if !strings.Contains(joinedGO, "GAME OVER") {
299			t.Error("QA-SD-7 game-over: GAME OVER overlay missing after ignored soft drop")
300		}
301	}

303	// QA-SD-8: Held soft drop descends continuously (one point per row accepted)
304	// NOTE: DAS timing is owned by the TUI input layer. This test verifies the game logic:
305	// each accepted PlayerSoftDrops() = one row + one point, stops at rest with no further points.
306	func TestQA_SD8_HeldSoftDropDescendsContinuously(t *testing.T) {
307		s := sdSession()
308		sdPieceNotAtRest(t, s, 0)
309		if t.Failed() {
310			return
311		}
312	
313		totalDrops := 0
314		for s.PlayerSoftDrops() {
315			totalDrops++
316		}
317		if totalDrops == 0 {
318			t.Fatal("QA-SD-8: no soft drops were accepted")
319		}
320		if s.Score != totalDrops {
321			t.Errorf("QA-SD-8: score = %d, want %d (one per accepted drop)", s.Score, totalDrops)
322		}
323		if !s.ActivePiece.AtRest(s.Board) {
324			t.Error("QA-SD-8: piece is not at rest after exhausting all soft drops")
325		}
326		// Extra press while at rest must not award points.
327		scoreBeforeExtra := s.Score
328		extraOk := s.PlayerSoftDrops()
329		if extraOk {
330			t.Error("QA-SD-8: soft drop accepted after piece reached rest — should be blocked")
331		}
332		if s.Score != scoreBeforeExtra {
333			t.Errorf("QA-SD-8: score changed during blocked attempt: score = %d, want %d", s.Score, scoreBeforeExtra)
334		}
335	}

337	// QA-SD-9 (Baseline — idle stability): no score change, no layout shift with no input.
338	func TestQA_SD9_IdleStabilityBaseline(t *testing.T) {
339		// Use waiting phase for a fully static frame.
340		s := game.NewSession(rand.New(rand.NewSource(42)))
341	
342		rows1 := sdFrame(s)
343		rows2 := sdFrame(s)
344	
345		if len(rows1) != len(rows2) {
346			t.Fatalf("QA-SD-9: frame height changed between renders: %d vs %d", len(rows1), len(rows2))
347		}
348		for i := range rows1 {
349			if rows1[i] != rows2[i] {
350				t.Errorf("QA-SD-9: frame row %d changed between consecutive renders (no input)", i)
351			}
352		}
353	
354		// Score must be zero — no spurious change.
355		if s.Score != 0 {
356			t.Errorf("QA-SD-9: score changed without input: got %d", s.Score)
357		}
358	
359		// No scrollback growth: frame does not use \n in a way that appends lines.
360		out := Render(s.Snapshot(), 40, 24).String()
361		if strings.HasSuffix(out, "\r\n") {
362			t.Error("QA-SD-9: frame output ends with \\r\\n — trailing newline causes scrollback growth")
363		}
364	}

366	// --- Rendering invariants for soft drop ---
367	
368	// TestRenderSoftDropActivePieceMovesDown verifies that after a soft drop the
369	// active piece footprint is one row lower than before in the rendered frame.
370	func TestRenderSoftDropActivePieceMovesDown(t *testing.T) {
371		s := sdSession()
372		sdPieceNotAtRest(t, s, 3)
373		if t.Failed() {
374			return
375		}
376	
377		rsBefore := s.Snapshot()
378		beforeRows := map[int]bool{}
379		for _, p := range rsBefore.Active.Footprint {
380			if p.Row >= 0 {
381				beforeRows[p.Row] = true
382			}
383		}
384	
385		ok := s.PlayerSoftDrops()
386		if !ok {
387			t.Fatal("soft drop not accepted")
388		}
389	
390		rsAfter := s.Snapshot()
391		afterRows := map[int]bool{}
392		for _, p := range rsAfter.Active.Footprint {
393			if p.Row >= 0 {
394				afterRows[p.Row] = true
395			}
396		}
397	
398		// Every after-row must be exactly one greater than a before-row.
399		for r := range afterRows {
400			if !beforeRows[r-1] {
401				t.Errorf("after soft drop: piece at row %d has no corresponding row %d before drop", r, r-1)
402			}
403		}
404	}
405	
406	// TestRenderSoftDropGhostAnchored verifies that the ghost top row does not change
407	// after a soft drop (ghost stays at landing position).
408	func TestRenderSoftDropGhostAnchored(t *testing.T) {
409		s := sdSession()
410		sdPieceNotAtRest(t, s, 0)
411		if t.Failed() {
412			return
413		}
414	
415		rsBefore := s.Snapshot()
416		if rsBefore.Active == nil || len(rsBefore.Active.Ghost) == 0 {
417			t.Skip("no ghost before soft drop")
418		}
419		ghostMinBefore := 1<<31 - 1
420		for _, p := range rsBefore.Active.Ghost {
421			if p.Row < ghostMinBefore {
422				ghostMinBefore = p.Row
423			}
424		}
425	
426		ok := s.PlayerSoftDrops()
427		if !ok {
428			t.Fatal("soft drop not accepted")
429		}
430	
431		rsAfter := s.Snapshot()
432		if rsAfter.Active == nil || len(rsAfter.Active.Ghost) == 0 {
433			t.Skip("no ghost after soft drop")
434		}
435		ghostMinAfter := 1<<31 - 1
436		for _, p := range rsAfter.Active.Ghost {
437			if p.Row < ghostMinAfter {
438				ghostMinAfter = p.Row
439			}
440		}
441	
442		if ghostMinAfter != ghostMinBefore {
443			t.Errorf("ghost top row changed after soft drop: before=%d after=%d (ghost should stay at landing)", ghostMinBefore, ghostMinAfter)
444		}
445	}
